**The design for v5 (what you build):**

- `ui/floating-layer.ts` — `export const FLOATING_LAYER_ATTR = 'data-qt-floating-layer'`; `export function isInFloatingLayer(target: EventTarget | null): boolean` (v4's bytes). No `floatingLayerProps` (Angular binds the attribute statically: `data-qt-floating-layer=""`).
- `ui/floating-dialog.ts` — `qt-floating-dialog`, a standalone component rendered IN PLACE by its host (v5's convention — no portal; the `fixed` positioning makes the host position irrelevant; record it), with `<ng-content>` for the body and `<ng-content select="[qt-floating-dialog-actions]">` for the header actions; inputs `open = input.required<boolean>()`, `title = input.required<string>()`, `storageKey = input('quilltap:help-dialog-geometry')`, `initialGeometry = input<Partial<DialogGeometry>>({})`, `minWidth = input(320)`, `minHeight = input(300)`, `topmost = input(false, {transform: booleanAttribute})`; output `close`; the geometry state (`loadGeometry` / `saveGeometry` / `constrainToViewport` as PURE exported functions in the same file, v4's arithmetic byte-for-byte), the header drag (`mousedown` on `.qt-floating-dialog-header` ignoring `closest('button')`; document `mousemove` / `mouseup` bound only while open), the `ResizeObserver` with the 2-px threshold, the two Escape listeners EXACTLY as `FloatingDialog.tsx:175-196` (the element listener swallows when `topmost`; the document listener defers to any `[aria-modal="true"]` in the document — `qt-modal` renders `aria-modal="true"` (`ui/modal.ts:35`), so a `qt-modal` beneath the console keeps its Escape), `dff76ebef`'s R2 semantics (`role="dialog" aria-modal="false" [attr.aria-labelledby]=titleId tabindex="-1"`, a per-instance `titleId` from a module counter `qt-floating-dialog-title-<n>`, focus-in on open in a `requestAnimationFrame` unless the dialog already contains `document.activeElement`, the opener captured when `open` flips true and refocused on close if `isConnected`, NO Tab trap, the close button `title="Close" aria-label="Close"` with `<qt-icon name="close" class="w-4 h-4" />`), the classes `qt-floating-dialog` + `qt-floating-dialog-topmost` and the `data-qt-floating-layer` attribute when `topmost`.
```ts
export interface AbsentMentionCard { name: string; aliases: string[]; pronouns: string | null; body: string }
export interface AbsentMention { messageId: string; characterId: string; text: string; card: AbsentMentionCard }
export interface ChatAbsentMentionsRequest { type: 'chatAbsentMentions'; chatId: string }
export interface ChatAbsentMentionsResult { mentions: AbsentMention[] }
```

v5 `screens/about/about-page.ts:434-459` carries the list VERBATIM at an older vintage: its `Markdown & Documents` body (`:449-451`) is `unified, remark-parse, remark-gfm, remark-rehype, rehype-stringify, rehype-highlight, react-markdown, react-syntax-highlighter, mammoth, pdf-parse, PDF.js, yaml, MessagePack` — it lacks `remark-math`, `rehype-katex`, `remark-breaks`, `remark-smartypants`, `KaTeX`, `jsonrepair` (copied before v4 added them) and still names `pdf-parse`. The port REPLACES the whole string (not a one-token edit), inserts `{ label: 'Diagrams', body: '…' }` after it and `{ label: 'Terminals & Realtime', body: '…' }` after `UI & Interaction`; the resulting order is Core, Editor, AI & LLM, Markdown & Documents, Diagrams, UI & Interaction, Terminals & Realtime, Filesystem & Archives, Desktop & Infrastructure, Testing. The `:434` comment `/** v4 \`:362-369\`, verbatim. */` → `/** v4 \`AboutView.tsx:373-383\` at \`236c04e35\`, verbatim. */`. Ruling R-5 (below): verbatim stays the policy.

### I. The Inventory dialog's reloads (`0276d10d9` client) — P4.D350's HANDOFF

**v4 at the pin:** `lib/realtime/topic-map.ts:134-146` — the `inventory` topic returns `[inventory.lists, inventory.folderLists, inventory.archetypeLists, inventory.equippedLists]` ("An object file was written — through any Inventory location (the operator's dialog, a character's `inventory_*` tool, a Pascal effect), or by a job child's buffered write; an open dialog's lists, folders and archetype pickers re-read, and so does every Carrying-now pane, which shows the objects resolved. The dialog leaves its own writes' refresh to this"); `:35-40` the reconnect sweep adds the last two; `lib/hooks/use-inventory.ts` — `refreshUnlessRealtime(queryClient, keys)`: `if (getRealtimeStatus() === 'connected') return Promise.resolve(); return Promise.all(keys.map(invalidate))` ("After this tab's own write: refresh `keys` only while the realtime socket is down. While it is up, the server's publish for that write refreshes the same keys, and refreshing here as well would fetch everything twice."); the object-write `onSettled` → `refreshUnlessRealtime([lists, folderLists, archetypeLists, equippedLists])`; the carried-set write → `refreshUnlessRealtime([equipped(chatId), lists])`; `useInventoryDependents` DELETED with the `item` / `dependentLists` / `dependents` keys; `lib/inventory/container.ts:73-81` `isInContainer(item, container)`: `!item.origin → true`; `container.scope !== 'character' → item.origin.scope === container.scope`; else `item.origin.scope === 'character' && item.origin.id === container.id`; `use-inventory-vantage-items.ts` — `own = container ? list.items.filter(item => isInContainer(item, container)) : []`; `useInventoryListData.ts:16` — the "own" predicate becomes `container !== null && isInContainer(item, container)`.

**v5 today (the red):** `inventory/inventory-control-dialog.ts:345-351` — `realtime.onTopic('inventory', () => { void this.data.reload(); void this.folders.reload(); })` reloads the lists and the folder tree ONLY; the archetype pickers (the instantiate dialog's / the browser's archetype tier — measure which signal loaders hold them: `grep -n 'archetype' inventory/*.ts`) and the Carrying-now pane (`this.chat.reload()`, reloaded today only on the `chats` topic `:351`) are NOT re-read on `inventory`; `:354-357` `refresh()` after a write ALWAYS reloads data + folders + chat (v4 now refreshes its own writes only while the socket is down); `inventory/inventory-vantage-items.ts:49-54` `own = items.filter(item => item.origin ? item.origin.scope === scope : true)` — the PRE-fix predicate (a character vantage counts a GROUP-origin item as not-own correctly, but a character-origin item of ANOTHER character as own — the bug `isInContainer` fixes); `core/realtime-topic-map.ts:151-159` the `inventory` row returns `[]` with the R-2 comment (keep the shape; UPDATE the comment to name the four re-reads the dialog now performs); `core/realtime.service.ts:69` `connected` signal (the `getRealtimeStatus() === 'connected'` twin), `:175-177` `refetchInterval`. **The port:** the `inventory` subscription reloads `data`, `folders`, the archetype pickers AND `chat` (the Carrying-now pane); `refresh()` becomes `refreshUnlessRealtime()` — `if (realtime.connected()) return; …reload all…` — with the SAME keys v4 names; `isInContainer(item, container)` as an exported pure function in `inventory/inventory-container.ts` (the v5 home of `container.ts`'s twins — measure the file) consumed by the vantage loader's `pool.owns` (the loader keeps the whole `container`, not just its scope) and by the dialog's list-data "own" predicate (measure its v5 home: `grep -n 'origin.scope' inventory/*.ts`). The server side that makes the reload fire on an OPERATOR write is P4.D350's R-1 (the location-level publish); your spec drives `RealtimeService.onTopic('inventory')` directly and your e2e beat is gated `P4D350_PUBLISH_LANDED`.

### J. The WCAG rules on the files you own (contract C9; P4.D358's HANDOFFs to you)

## Tiered deliverables

Every item names its spec (the SPA's equivalence tests are component-harness
specs over the DOM + the dispatch payloads, and tier-1 corpora recorded from
v4's REAL pure modules at the pin — §R.4), its red-first measurement, and the
gate constant it hides behind when a server contract is not in your tree.
Spec files sit beside their components (`<name>.spec.ts`); the harness is the
existing vitest + `@testing-library/angular`-free `TestBed` idiom of
`brahma/brahma-console-dialog.spec.ts:1-100` (read it first — memory note
`spa-component-harness-gotchas`: `RealtimeService` / `injectQuery` need the
stream stub + a TanStack provider; dispatch errors carry no status; render
between input + blur; `retry: false`).

   production entry `markMentionsInHtml(html: string, mentions:
   ReadonlyArray<{text: string}>): string` which parses `html` with
   `DOMParser` (`text/html`, the body's children), builds the same `pending`
   list, walks DOM text nodes with THIS eligibility rule (`MENTION_MARKERS_
   POST_RENDER`, ruling R-2): a text node is eligible iff its nearest
   block-level ancestor is one of `p, h1–h6, li, blockquote` and every
   element between the text node and that block is a `<span>` the roleplay
   tokenizer emits (any `<span>` whose class list contains NO class starting
   with `katex` / `hljs`); any `code`, `pre`, `a`, `em`, `strong`, `del`,
   `s`, `sub`, `sup`, `kbd`, `img` or katex / hljs span on the path makes the
   node ineligible (v4 skips those by never descending into non-eligible
   ELEMENTS: in its PRE-tokenizer tree the only element children of a `p`
   are markdown's inline elements, and the roleplay spans do not exist yet
   — the roleplay tokenizer runs on the TEXT the plugin already marked, so
   the marked run lands inside the dialogue span; v5's post-render walk
   must therefore see THROUGH those spans and nothing else); then — the chip
   emission v4 does in `withMentionChips` — each marked run in an eligible
   text node is replaced by `<span class="qt-mention-chip" tabindex="0"
   role="button" aria-label="<name>, not in this conversation"
   data-mention="<index>"><text></span>` where `<name>` is `mentions[index].
   card.name` HTML-escaped (so `markMentionsInHtml` takes the FULL mention
   objects, not just `{text}`); the private-use markers never reach the
   output (the MentionChip test's `not.toMatch(/[-]/)`
   assertion is the fence); the serialized body's `innerHTML` is returned;
   with NO mentions the input string is returned UNCHANGED (reference-equal
   — no parse). `off-scene-kinds.ts`: the three constants + `isOffScene
   MentionIntroduction(m: {systemSender?: string | null; systemKind?:
   string | null; content?: string | null})` (v4's bytes). **The corpus

   write under `harness/oracle/cases/mention-markers.ts`? — NO: the harness
   tree is a Rust-side asset; the SPA's recorders live under
   `apps/web/tooling/` (the `capture-markdown-fixtures.mts` precedent
   P4.D352 names) — `apps/web/tooling/record-mention-markers.mts`, run with
   `cd "$PIN" && npx tsx <v5>/apps/web/tooling/record-mention-markers.mts >
   <v5>/apps/web/src/testing/fixtures/mention-markers.oracle.ndjson`):**

   / `null`: the five `name matcher` cases + a grid: Unicode letters each

## The differential requirement, restated for this lane

This lane touches no crate, so it owns NO `quilltap-harness` family and runs
NO sweep (`harness/tools/recipe_sweep.py` has nothing of yours; do not invent
a recipe). Its equivalence proof is the SPA's own three-layer discipline:

1. **Tier-1 SPA corpora recorded from v4's REAL code at the pin** (§R.4): the
   two NDJSON fixtures `apps/web/src/testing/fixtures/mention-markers.
   oracle.ndjson` (item 8) and `pronoun-guidance.oracle.ndjson` (item 15),
   each produced by a tsx recorder under `apps/web/tooling/` that `import`s
   the pin's `lib/chat/mention-markers.ts` + `lib/chat/name-mentions.ts` /
   `lib/wardrobe/pronoun-guidance.ts` + `lib/characters/pronoun-presets.ts`
   by RELATIVE path into `$PIN` (never `@/…` resolved against the live
   checkout; never the v4 working tree), run as `cd "$PIN" && PATH=$HOME/
   .nvm/versions/node/v24.13.1/bin:$PATH npx tsx ~/source/quilltap-v5/apps/
   web/tooling/record-<name>.mts > ~/source/quilltap-v5/apps/web/src/
   testing/fixtures/<name>.oracle.ndjson`; the precedents are
   `apps/web/src/app/pascal/custom-tool-types.corpus.spec.ts` with its
   fixture README (`src/testing/fixtures/README.md` — add a section per
   fixture: provenance sha `236c04e35`, the row counts by kind, the date,
   the recorder's path) and P4.D346's `tool-draft-grammar` recorder of this
   round. Each row carries `kind`, `input`, `expected` (the v4 output
   `JSON.stringify`'d); the replay spec byte-compares. **The silent-stale-
   pass discipline (ledger §5.2):** check the fresh NDJSON is non-empty and
   its row count matches the README BEFORE believing a green replay; a
   replay that passes against a zero-row file is a test of nothing; commit
   the fixture in the SAME commit as the twin it proves.
2. **Transcribed v4 jest cases** where v4 shipped them (`FloatingDialog-
   topmost.test.tsx` ×4, `useClickOutside.test.ts`'s floating-layer ×3,
   `MentionChip.test.tsx` ×3, `mention-markers.test.ts` ×4 describes) —
   each spec header cites the v4 file:line it transcribes.
3. **Hand-written component specs for hunks v4 shipped without tests**
   (the keystone UI, the About strings, the cache writes, the Inspector
   tables, the Humanoid checkbox, the pronoun UI, the Inventory reloads),
   each asserting v4's BYTES (strings, classes, `title`s, dispatch
   payloads) read from the pin, never from this order's paraphrase.

SPA / theme case; the WCAG survey §5 confirms). **Oracle cases this lane

## Rulings made at planning

Items the HUMAN must rule on are marked ⚖ and come first; the rest are the
planner's calls (the alternative and why).

- ⚖ **R-1 — BUILD the scoped `qt-floating-dialog` primitive (the round's
  R-K; WCAG survey §10 #2).** v4's `99463fc56` + `dff76ebef` make the
  console a NON-modal, topmost, draggable, geometry-persisting panel usable
  over an open form; v5's scrim modal cannot carry that, and a launcher
  that opens a modal over a modal is a regression. The CSS has existed
  unused in v5 since P4.d17 (`_surfaces.css:279-323`). SCOPED: the console
  only this round — the Inform / compose-mail / insert-announcement / Help
  dialogs stay `qt-modal` (the `status-log.md:41019-41022` ruling is
  overturned for the console and RE-AFFIRMED for those four until a later
  order moves them). **Alternative (the human may choose):** keep the scrim
  modal and record `99463fc56`'s floating half as a divergence — then
  Tier 3 item 32 applies and the launcher is NOT ported.
- ⚖ **R-2 — the two ADAPTER divergences of the chips, recorded by name.**
  `MENTION_MARKERS_POST_RENDER`: v4 marks text nodes in the rehype tree
  BEFORE the roleplay tokenizer; v5 renders to an HTML string, so the pass
  walks the RENDERED DOM's text nodes and sees through the tokenizer's
  `<span>`s (the eligibility rule in item 8). `MENTION_CARD_SHARED_POPOVER`:
  v4 wraps each chip in a `Tooltip interactive pinnable delay={250}`; v5
  cannot wrap a span inside `[innerHTML]`, so ONE card component is
  positioned under the active chip by a host directive with the same
  delay / grace / pin rules. Both are proven on the recorded corpus + the
  three transcribed MentionChip cases; both are named in the lane record
  with the one shape each could diverge on. **Alternative:** re-render each
  message through an Angular template tree (a React-style node renderer)
  — a rewrite of P4.D352's renderer this round cannot carry.
- **R-3 — the chips' stacking path.** PRE-START from `main` on everything;
  the ONE hook commit after a rebase onto P4.D353's Tier-1 commit; if that
  commit is not on any branch when the rest is green, the hook becomes a
  patch-file HANDOFF and the chips stay gated (the preamble). Alternative:
  wait for P4.D353 — rejected (a lane idle on a sibling is the §R.9 cap
  wasted).
- **R-4 — the floating-layer guard goes on EVERY document-level dismiss
  site (36), not only the five the planner's row names.** v4 put the guard
  in the ONE hook every site uses; v5 has thirty-six hand-rolled listeners,
  and an unguarded one lets a click in the console close a dialog beneath
  — the exact regression the primitive exists to avoid. The committed guard
  table (item 3) is the tripwire for the next listener someone adds.
  Alternative: the five sites only — rejected on the census.
- **R-5 — the About credits stay VERBATIM v4 (WCAG survey §10 #5),** incl.
  the `Diagrams` line, which is true of v5 only if P4.D353's R-B lands the
  four client deps; the human may instead ask for v5's own stack
  (Angular, ProseMirror, markdown-it, …) — then the About page becomes a v5
  authored text with its own policy note; recommended: verbatim now, one
  source of truth, revisit when the About page is redesigned.
- **R-6 — `2041bb875` is P4.D346's line** (it owns `automation-settings.ts`
  this round and its order claims the hunk by name); you record the
  HANDOFF and verify on the union. Alternative: you edit the file —
  rejected (two lanes in one file for one class string).
- **R-7 — the chat-start composer's pronoun toggle is RECORDED, not
  ported** (item 24): v5's Compose mode is disabled behind the deferred
  wardrobe-composer family; a toggle beside a disabled composer would
  filter nothing. Alternative: lift the deferral — out of scope.
- **R-8 — the `['chat-settings']` / `['chatSettings']` split CONVERGES on
  `chatSettingsKeys.all`** with v4's `setQueryData`-from-response shape
  (item 13). Alternative: keep two keys and invalidate both — rejected (two
  spellings of one row is the bug class v4's commit is about).
- **R-9 — `LlmLogType` widens to v4's 25 in v4's order, incl. `STORYTELLER_
  BID`, `INVENTORY_ITEM_IMAGE`, `CUSTOM_TOOL_CONSULT`, `VOICE_REWRITE`,** so
  the derived `other` can be derived at all (I1 needs the VALUE list); the
  `:25-28` lag comment retires; P4.D344 adds the `storyteller` GROUP only.
- **R-10 — no new dependency.** Everything here is template + CSS + pure
  TS; `package-lock.json` does not move.
- **R-11 — `refreshUnlessRealtime` in v5 reads `RealtimeService.connected()`**
  (`realtime.service.ts:69`) — the one status signal every fallback gate
  already reads; the dialog's own-write refresh is skipped while connected
  because P4.D350's R-1 publish (the server side) now reaches the dialog's
  `inventory` subscription. If P4.D350's R-1 is OVERRULED by the human (its
  `PUBLISH_AFTER_COMMIT` table), the dialog's own-write refresh must stay
  unconditional — a one-line flip your lane record names as the dependency.
- **R-12 — the private-use markers are `` / `` / ``

  custom-tool LLM consult can narrate a verdict the table already reached
  (bug 257)", v4 main 2026-10-20 16:50 −0500, `4.11.0-dev.53`). **This
  round is PINNED at `236c04e35`** = `236c04e35ab23ccc604fd188e5b73d0141066697`
  ("dev: started 4.12.0 development", v4 main 2026-10-28 19:30 −0500,
  `package.json` `"version": "4.12.0-dev.0",`; `packages/quilltap/
  package.json` `4.11.0-dev.112` — the CLI package's stamp lags, grep each
  file). **The pin's TREE is code-identical to v4's 4.11.0 release** (tag
  `4.11.0` = `1ca45c171`, whose code is `7789ebed6`'s; `git diff 7789ebed6
  236c04e35 -- lib app packages plugins help components` is EMPTY) — so
  "the 4.11.0 code" and "the pin" are the same bytes, and the planner chose
  the first-parent commit over the tag because `git log <tag>..main` lists
  the whole 4.10 → 4.11 squash (the tag's sole parent is the 4.10.0
  release) while `git log 236c04e35..main` lists exactly what is past the
  pin. The pin is NOT v4 HEAD at planning: HEAD was `bf7a457f6`, two CODE
  commits past it (`8832cfd20` moves v4's SQLCipher driver to
  `better-sqlite3-multiple-ciphers` 13.0.3 — SQLite3MC 2.4.0 / SQLite
  3.53.4 where v5 links 2.3.5 / 3.53.2, a RULING owed before any pin
  crosses it; `bf7a457f6` the iCloud scan fix), and the live checkout was
  DIRTY mid-edit. **The human's ruling: the work follows the PIN, not the
  latest commit or later changes in v4.** Every lane ports `236c04e35`'s
  code as `236c04e35`'s code. Spec reads are `git -C ~/source/quilltap-server
  show 236c04e35:<path>` for a pin file, `git show <sha> -- <path>` for one
  commit's hunk, `show 322a5488e:<path>` for the PRE shape v5 ported, `git
  diff 322a5488e 236c04e35 -- <paths>` for the span. Never the commit
  message, never an order's prose, **never the working tree** (it is past
  the pin and dirty).
- **§R.2 — The freshness probe is an OBSERVATION, not a gate (the pin
  rule).** Run the drift ledger's §2 probe at lane start and record its
  output. `log 236c04e35..main` is NOT empty today (`8832cfd20`,
  `bf7a457f6`, and whatever lands later) and the tree is DIRTY (the
  chokidar removal in `lib/file-storage/watcher.ts` / `lib/mount-index/
  watcher.ts` / `package.json`) — EXPECTED; RECORD ("v4 moved: `<sha>`
  observed at `<time>`; tree dirty: `<files>`") and CONTINUE — the pin
  worktree (§R.3) is unaffected. **STOP only when the PIN is unusable:**
  `git -C "$PIN" rev-parse HEAD` is not `236c04e35…`; or `git show
  236c04e35:package.json` does not read `4.12.0-dev.0`; or the pin's root
  `node_modules` cannot be made to match the pin's lockfile (§R.3's cure
  fails). A lane never writes the ledger, never re-pins, never ports a hunk
  newer than `236c04e35`.
- **§R.3 — Regen rule: PIN REQUIRED at `236c04e35`** (ledger §5.1). The
  baseline `322a5488e` is behind the target; the live checkout is past it
  AND dirty.
  - **The pin.** Every lane regenerates from a LANE-UNIQUE detached pin:
    `git -C ~/source/quilltap-server worktree add --detach
    /tmp/qt-v4-pin-<orderid-lowercase>-236c04e35 236c04e35`. Add the THREE
    symlink classes (root `node_modules`, `packages/quilltap/node_modules`,
    every `plugins/dist/*/node_modules`), then `cd` into it. Never stash or
    checkout-switch the human's repo; `git worktree list` before each batch.
    (The planner's read-only survey worktree `/tmp/qt-v4-pin-setupphase-
    7789ebed6` is NOT a regen pin — never run an oracle from it.)
  - **Verify the pin** by `git -C "$PIN" rev-parse HEAD` AND `ls -ld`; build
    every pin path from a quoted literal; check a fresh NDJSON is non-empty
    before believing a diff.

  - **Node.** `PATH=$HOME/.nvm/versions/node/v24.13.1/bin:$PATH` (a REAL path

  - **The sweep driver** is the sanctioned per-family path: `cd <the lane's

    lane's own worktree (`~/source/quilltap-v5/.claude/worktrees/<orderid-

    machine: never two sweeps at once; a lane announces a sweep to the
    human before starting it.** A family reporting `SKIP` in 0.00 s did not
    run. The system python 3.9 cannot parse the driver. The driver does
    NOT rewrite a recipe's `/tmp` outputs (memory note
    `parallel-lane-shared-resources` §3) — copy outputs to a lane-private
    dir right after a sweep.

  - (h) **The keystone-memory facts the row overstated:** `keystone` is a
    FLAG, not a tier (R-E) — `bucketCandidates` skips `memory.keystone`
    rows AFTER `rowsLoaded++`, BEFORE the `CONSOLIDATED` branch; `isDemotable
    = hot && AUTO && !keystone`; `policyProtectionOf` answers `'keystone'`
    FIRST and `sparedBy`'s key order becomes `{keystone, manual, digest,
    cold}`; a keystone fold survivor takes NO footnotes and an EMPTY patch
    but keeps links + the reinforcement delta; `cascadeEmptiedDigests` keeps
    a keystone digest whose last member retires (DEBUG `Keystone digest kept
    though its last member retires`) and its `added` now counts only rows
    actually doomed (`if (!grew) break`) — port as shipped; `protection()`
    answers `{score: 1, protected: true, protectedBySource: true}`. In
    recall, "every digest is cut before any keystone" holds only INSIDE the
    inner circle (`innerCircleTrimOrder` sorts `Number(b.keystone) −
    Number(a.keystone)` first): present-character keystones are LIFTED into
    the circle at `tier −1` ahead of that character's digests, self
    keystones join the anchors, and `renderArchive` / `renderHead` render a
    keystone's `content` first (`summary` first otherwise). `KEYSTONES_PER_
    SUBJECT_MAX = 5`; the v4 quirk — `POST /memories` with `keystone: true`
    answers 400 `The memory was saved, but not as a keystone: <msg>` AFTER
    writing the row — is ported as the bytes (Q3). `keystone` appears in
    EVERY parsed Memory (between `consolidatedAt` and `createdAt`), so every
    byte pin of a memory row moves; `hydrateRawRows` coerces number / bigint
    / null to a boolean and DROPS a string cell with the WARN.
  - (i) **The WCAG ownership rule** (wcag survey §7.3): "the lane with the
    larger or structural hunk owns the file; the a11y lane records a HANDOFF
    naming the rule(s) R1–R12 and v4's bytes; the owner applies them in its
    own commit; the unifier checks the handoff list. Ties go to the feature
    lane (the a11y edits are mechanical)." Every sibling SPA lane applies
    rules **R1–R12** (the binding text is Shared contract C9) to the files it
    owns and records each as a `HANDOFF:` for P4.D358's census; P4.D358's

- **§R.5 — Log lines.** Every line this round ports or moves lands with a
  capture pin (the thread-scoped rig) AND its silence leg.
  - A line emitted INSIDE a `Db::write` closure runs on the writer thread and
    is INVISIBLE to the rig: run the home on the caller thread (memory note
    `capture-rig-writer-thread`) or use `global_capture`.
  - A `db::fallback` line renders the BARE SQLite / Zod message
    (`error_text`), never `sqlite error: …`.
  - A structured v4 field that is an array or object renders through the
    `…Json` file-layer convention; v4's camelCase keys are the keys; an
    `undefined` v4 field is OMITTED; child-logger bindings are fields on the
    line; `durationMs` and other wall-clock fields are MASKED.
  - Open before `captured_with`; a v4 catch UNREACHABLE through v4's real
    code is pinned NEGATIVE with a v5 fallback home.
  - **Migration lines follow the standing precedent** (P4.D249 / P4.D304 /
    P4.D315 / P4.D324, `host.rs`): v4's migration INFO / ERROR texts are
    NO-PORT and each boot ensure logs ONE v5-worded INFO per added column /
    table / index.

- **§R.7 — Red-first.**
  - **Measure red before touching source** — on unported `main` for the
    wave-0 lanes, on the `KEYSTONE` tip for its dependents (a contract
    refusal arm answering `not_available` IS the red). The lane record
    carries the red count per family. A test that passes before the fix is
    a test of nothing.

    `FRESH_STORE_RESIDUAL` / `BOOT_CREATED_HISTORY_TABLE`, so a v4
    convergence trips them. This round's planned names: **P4.D345**
    `HOLD_KEEP_ALIVE_NO_STREAM` (v4's 15 s keep-alive ping — no v5
    counterpart by architecture), `ROUND_DROPS_MIN_EXCLUSIVE` (only if the
    corpus shows it); **P4.D354** `REFUSING_SEAM_LEAVES_PDF_UNEXTRACTED`

    + `SENTENCE_SLICE_SURROGATE` (a JS `slice` that cuts a surrogate pair —

- **§R.8 — The unifier's accumulation.** Each lane's record states its
  `Cargo.toml` versions at close; the unifier takes the HIGHEST and bumps +1
  for the union commit; `Cargo.lock` follows. The SPA: the highest of the
  SPA lanes, +1. CHANGELOG entries and `status-log.md` lane records
  union-merge (append-only).
- **§R.9 — One machine.**
  - **Workspace gates:** at most TWO Rust workspace gates at once. Count them
    with ONE `ps -ax -o pid,etime,command | grep "cargo test --workspace" |
    grep -v grep`; if two run, queue yours in ONE background command waiting
    on the siblings' PIDs (`while kill -0 <pidA> && kill -0 <pidB>; do sleep
    20; done; sleep $((RANDOM % 20)); <re-count once; if still two, wait on
    those PIDs>; cargo test …`), never on a pattern (memory notes
    `no-pattern-kill-on-shared-machine`, `parallel-lane-shared-resources`
    §2 — co-waiters all fire on one exit; re-count after the wait).
  - **Sweeps and Playwright:** one sweep at a time (announced); one
    Playwright run at a time (lanes by file only; the full suite is the
    unifier's). New own-instance e2e files' server ports are unique per
    lane (4319–4340, 4343–4350, 4352 and mocks 45301–45304, 45340–45348 are
    taken): **P4.D344 4353 / mock 45351**, **P4.D346 4354 / mock 45352**,
    **P4.D352 4355 / mock 45353**, **P4.D353 4356 / mock 45354**, **P4.D358
    4357 / mock 45355**, **P4.D359 4358 / mock 45356**, **P4.D355 4359 /
    mock 45357** (only if a terminal e2e is added). A lane that only edits
    an EXISTING spec takes no port.
  - **Long jobs:** `run_in_background: true`, full log in a lane-private
    file, awaited by the completion notification — NEVER a `pgrep`/`grep`
    poll loop (CLAUDE.md), never `tail -N` on a gate log. A gate reads its
    oracles and source at RUN time: no regen and no source edit while one
    runs (memory note `lane-gate-hygiene`).
  - **Targets:** clone main's `target/` before the first build (`cp -cR`;
    `/carryout` rule 8 — same volume only, memory note
    `apfs-clone-same-volume-only`); main's `target/` was warmed by the
    `/setupphase` chain on 2026-10-29 at the orders' commit (the test /
    clippy set the lanes clone; the release bins were swept — an
    own-instance e2e that prefers `target/release/*` must delete the stale
    release bins from its clone or build release, memory note
    `lane-spa-env-node-modules-and-release-bins`); a stacked lane may clone
    its parent lane's worktree target instead, when that lane is not
    mid-build.
  - **`apps/web/dist/`:** a fresh worktree has none — build it before any
    workspace clippy / test. **`apps/web/node_modules`:** `npm ci` in the
    lane (a cloned copy fails every vitest spec with NG0203).
  - **Concurrency:** the cap is the two-gate rule, not the lane count;
    twenty lanes do NOT all run at once (§S.0 waves). Disk at planning:
    144 GiB free on the repo volume with `target/` at 347 GB BEFORE the
    warm sweep — the human sizes the waves from the post-sweep numbers in
    the hand-off report.

- **§R.14 — Stacking rules.** Each named stacking commit — `KEYSTONE`
  (P4.D341), `CONTEXT` (P4.D347), `SPINE` (P4.D348), `CATALOG` (P4.D350's
  FIRST commit), `FILES` (P4.D354) — is ONE commit whose subject starts with
  its name; the owner records the sha in its status header ON ITS LANE
  BRANCH the moment it lands. A dependent lane — P4.D347 / P4.D342 / P4.D351
  / P4.D356 from `KEYSTONE`; P4.D348 from `CONTEXT`; P4.D343 / P4.D345 from
  `SPINE` (which is on `CONTEXT` on `KEYSTONE`); P4.D349 from `CATALOG`;
  P4.D355 from `FILES` — finds the sha per `/carryout` rule 2 (`git log --all
  --format='%h %s' | grep -E '^[0-9a-f]+ <NAME>:'`, confirmed against the
  parent's lane-branch copy of the order) and branches from it — never from
  `main`, never a guessed sha; if the parent has not recorded it, STOP and
  report. A stacked lane may PRE-START from `main` on work that calls none
  of its parent's contract names — P4.D342's prompt bytes (the four
  extraction ladders + the consolidation tail) + the inner-circle resolver
  (`services/inner_circle.rs`, the tuning knobs); P4.D343's `tools/effects.rs`
  + `pascal/tool_effect.rs` + `StreamParams.tool_choice` + the `tool-build`
  re-aim (its unit E) + the pure `storyteller/*` modules; P4.D345's hold
  registry + eligibility / overrides / recompute / spans + the engine's
  `ReplayInputs` + the ONE corpus regen + the schema re-vendor; P4.D351's
  `services/annotations.rs` + `template_prompt_hint.rs` ports; P4.D356's
  NanoGPT half + the Tier R verify; P4.D349's `pronoun-presets` /
  `pronoun-guidance` ports + the image-prompt `humanoid` threading; P4.D355's
  `db/log_entries.rs` + the terminal host; P4.D348's `operator_post.rs` /
  `whisper_send.rs` / `turn_control.rs` additions and the funnel recorder;
  P4.D347 has no pre-start (its first unit widens `RawMessage`, which rides
  the keystone's row shapes) — rebasing onto the parent's commit before the
  first commit that does call a contract name; that rebase is the one
  sanctioned exception to "no rebase mid-lane". Two sibling-stacked parts
  (not lane-named commits): P4.D346's grammar-twin part (`pascal/custom-tool-
  types.ts` + the corpus copy + the spec header) stacks on P4.D345's Tier-1
  commit, or authors blind and lets the unifier run `custom-tool-types.
  corpus.spec.ts` — the order states which; P4.D359's chips stack on
  P4.D353's Tier-1 commit (the `message-content.ts` hook). Every other lane
  cuts from `main` at the orders' commit.

## §S — Unifier wires (what no single lane can do)

- **§S.0 — Waves (the recommended execution; the two-gate cap rules the
  machine, not the lane count).**
  - **Wave 0, from the orders' commit, at once:** P4.D341 (→ `KEYSTONE`,
    the Opus / Fable tier), P4.D350 (→ `CATALOG` as its FIRST commit, then
    its own units), P4.D354 (→ `FILES`), and in parallel P4.D360, P4.D357,
    P4.D358. That is six worktrees.
  - **Wave 1, as slots free (still from the orders' commit):** the five SPA
    feature lanes P4.D344, P4.D346, P4.D352, P4.D353, P4.D359 (each authors
    against the contracts with gated beats; P4.D346's grammar-twin part and
    P4.D359's chips wait for their sibling's Tier-1 commit).
  - **Wave A, from `KEYSTONE`:** P4.D347 (→ `CONTEXT`) FIRST, then P4.D342,
    P4.D351, P4.D356 (its NanoGPT + Tier R half may have PRE-STARTED from
    `main`). **From `CATALOG`:** P4.D349 — FIRST among the independents
    (R-H). **From `FILES`:** P4.D355.
  - **Wave B, from `CONTEXT`:** P4.D348 (→ `SPINE`).
  - **Wave C, from `SPINE`:** P4.D343 and P4.D345 (the two turn-side
    consumers every later union depends on).

  Within a wave every lane compiles against the C sections below it alone.
- **§S.1 — The pick order.** P4.D341 → P4.D350 → P4.D349 → P4.D347 →
  P4.D348 → P4.D343 → P4.D345 → P4.D342 → P4.D351 → P4.D356 → P4.D354 →
  P4.D355 → P4.D357 → P4.D344 → P4.D346 → P4.D352 → P4.D353 → P4.D359 →
  P4.D360 → P4.D358. The reasons per adjacency: `KEYSTONE` first (every
  server lane's row shapes, the dumps, the ensures); P4.D350 next because
  `CATALOG` is the base P4.D349 branches from, and P4.D349 right after it
  (R-H — the three LIVE-DATA erasure writers land before anything else
  touches a vault); P4.D347 → P4.D348 → P4.D343 → P4.D345 in their stacking
  order (`CONTEXT` on `KEYSTONE`, `SPINE` on `CONTEXT`, the two consumers of
  S1 / S2 / C1 / `TurnStopReach` on `SPINE`; P4.D343 before P4.D345 so
  `trigger_runner.rs`'s pre-run ceiling block precedes the post-run `Vetoed`
  arm and `realtime/types.rs` lands `storytellerRounds` NINTH before
  `customToolInterventions` TENTH); P4.D342 and P4.D351 (keystone-only
  consumers, no spine seam) after the spine chain; P4.D356 after P4.D345 so
  the `customToolIntervention` arm it writes for P4.D345 and the
  `punctuationWarnings` key it writes for P4.D352 are in the tree when their
  families re-run, and after P4.D351 so `settings-routes` is regenerated
  ONCE at the end of the server picks; P4.D354 → P4.D355 (`FILES` then the
  two bridge sites switched); P4.D357 before every SPA lane (the CSS lines
  the fenced appends sit beside); P4.D344 before P4.D346 (`_index.css`
  imports in v4's order; `RealtimeTopicAppend`'s SPA half NINTH then TENTH;
  `LibraryEntryKeys`' SPA badges in v4's order); P4.D352 before P4.D353
  (`renderMarkdownToHtml` is P4.D352's; P4.D353 post-passes its HTML);
  P4.D353 before P4.D359 (P4.D359's chips stack on P4.D353's `message-
  content.ts` hook; its `_variables.css` append sits after P4.D353's);
  P4.D360 LATE (its help / docs guards judge a union carrying every server
  feature; its schema-guard CHECK sees the final constants; the P4.D339
  precedent: fourteenth) — nineteenth here, before P4.D358; P4.D358 LAST
  (its census allowlist for sibling-owned HANDOFF files is emptied against
  the finished union; its toast-selector first commit is cherry-picked
  EARLIER by any sibling that edits one of the 20 spec files).

  run maintenance on a copy v4 keystoned. **Terminals:** kill sends SIGHUP

  `messageId` NULL, the token summary unmoved, the table delta `storyteller_
  rounds` + `llm_logs` only; tick two → `Run in order` → depths 1 and 2 and
  `Since you planned it, <A> has spoken.`; a Lookup bid calling
  `doc_write_file` → `Not run: …`; `All at once` with concurrent bubbles;
  `@Name:`, whisper and nudge open no round; leaving the mode folds the
  board; a NanoGPT / DeepSeek bid request carries `"tool_choice":"none"`);
  💸 **an intervention hold on a real Pascal tool with a consult** (the
  dialog `Pascal holds: <title>`, `As rolled`, `Deal it` → the badge hover
  `Adjusted before it was dealt: …`, veto plain vs transparent texts, Stop
  while held (if `TurnStopReach` landed), reload reappears, two holds
  queued, Re-ask spending ONE `CUSTOM_TOOL_CONSULT` row, a hidden consult's
  compact section, the spans hints, the Automation toggle off, the 10-minute
  unattended hold over HTTP and Tauri, an autonomous room dealing as rolled);
  **a keystone memory surviving a consolidation + a backup / restore** (mark,
  `Keystones only`, a sixth refused with v4's sentence, a cold row refused;
  💸 recalled in FULL under `## Those Closest to You` / `## Memory Anchors`;
  💸 the inner circle's `tiers: 2` over a two-group holder; maintenance
  never folds / demotes it; `sparedBy` `{keystone, manual, digest, cold}`;
  a `.qtap` round trip and a `replace` restore keep it; `quilltap recall-
  replay --tuning '{"innerCircleShare":0.5,"innerCircleAllowance":[2]}'`;
  bash completion offers `keystone`); 💸 **a whisper that no longer chains**
  (one reply during a running chain, saved after it closes; one reply on an
  idle chat); 💸 **every Salon turn in the Inspector** (a stopped turn's row
  with partial content and no error; a provider error's row; a swipe's row
  under the new swipe's id; an `IMAGE_GENERATION` row from the Images route
  and in the Almanack's spend; the `Other` filter listing every unclaimed
  type); 💸 **the fair-rotation pause recording** a staged tool result, `roll
  2d6` and an image upload (TOOL rows, the rng row, the Librarian note); a
  paused chat with a keyless profile records the post; 💸 bug 259's whole
  attachment on every character's first turn; 💸 bug 263 / 264 / 265 row
  counts; 💸 **an absent character's name chip** (first mention, the hover
  card matching the ledger's card, `## Who is being spoken of` + `## The
  line you are answering` in the request, the old Host card no longer
  posting, a Continue Elsewhere notice with `off-scene-left-behind`; the
  dangling-pronoun line resolved); 💸 **a Novel-template chat** (narration
  italic unmarked, dialogue upright, `**` dropped, `((aside))` OOC, `* * *`
  a rule, multi-paragraph unclosed speech), the template editor copying
  Novel (`None — plain prose`, `Style unmarked text`, the asterisks box
  unticked), a `.qtap` export / import and a `replace` restore keeping
  `null` narration + `unmarkedStyle` + `markdownAsterisks: false`; **a
  punctuation warning in the composer** (a nested `"` lights up, hover
  explains; unclosed `((` after the caret leaves; the toggle off removes
  warnings but keeps the prose preview; Bold absent; the setting persists);
  💸 **a diagram fence rendered** (if R-B lands: Draws diagrams ON → the
  `[FORMATTING: DIAGRAMS]` note, a ```` ```mermaid ```` flowchart hand-drawn
  → Clean re-renders ruled, a ```` ```dot ```` graph, the lightbox zoom +
  Copy PNG into the composer, Escape returns the transcript, a theme switch
  re-draws, quick-hide leaves diagrams visible); **the vault keys** (a v4
  `"humanoid": false` surviving a v5 PUT / Descriptions save / 💸 Wizard
  regen; 💸 a non-humanoid avatar `its whole form in frame.`; a flipped box
  recompiling seated chats; the H&S backfill skipping it; v4-written
  `canDrawDiagrams: true, diagramStyle: 'clean'` surviving a rename; a
  `pronounGuidance: [she, they]` item surviving an edit and hidden from
  he/him's `wardrobe_list` with the trailing note; 💸 the green room's
  valet); `self_inventory`'s `## Core Vault Files` + `## Holdings`; the
  text-block `[[CREATE_WARDROBE_ITEM … cue="…"]]`; `POST …/inventory?action=
  duplicate` with `{}` → 400; an operator REST create refreshing a second
  open dialog; **a backup carrying the Log** (`data/log-entries.json` with
  `counts.logEntries` = `SELECT count(*)`, a `replace` restore keeping every
  line, a `new-account` restore remapping, a degraded logs DB → the warning,
  "Delete all my data" emptying `log_entries`, the backup still without
  `terminal_sessions`); **a terminal kill that lands** (bash exits on
  SIGHUP; a `sleep 9999` shell → the SIGKILL WARN after 3 s; deleting a chat
  with a live Ariel → `[PTY] Terminated sessions of deleted chat` + the rows
  gone on a v5-provisioned instance; the daily `Swept orphaned terminal
  transcripts` after the 1 h grace); **the files round** (💸 `doc_read_file`
  / `doc_grep` / `doc_list_files` with `**/CHANGELOG.md` and `*.{md,txt}` on
  attached uploads; the first-boot backfill INFOs and `background_jobs`
  rows — 💸 if the embedding profile is remote; the Docker folder picker at
  `/app/quilltap`; `quilltap sync --dry-run` refusal in the container;
  `docker exec quilltap quilltap db --lock-status`; a corrupt sibling → a
  MOUNT_CHUNK job RETRIES; 💸 the wizard's PDF import still logs the new
  WARN text); **the settings smalls** (`PUT /settings/chat {"coreWhisper":
  {"enabled":false}}` both transports; `{"foo":1}` → `Invalid settings:
  unknown key foo`; a planted `cheapLLMSettings = 5` → ONE row, the
  existing id with defaults + the WARN, the save writing the repair back;
  `quilltap db --count chats` under `FORCE_COLOR=1` a bare number); 💸
  **NanoGPT image sizes fitted** (a portrait avatar on a tier model posts
  `size: 1k, aspect_ratio: 2:3`; a non-square turned down → the square retry
  WARN and a square picture — the human's authorization, the P4.D283 C5
  precedent); **the WCAG pass** (six packs × light / dark, the toasts, the
  queue badges; reduced-motion; VoiceOver: `Skip to content`, the Salon
  `<h1>`, 💸 one real turn announced `<name> replied: …`, five dialogs'
  title + role + Escape + focus return; keyboard-only F6 out of Ariel,
  Enter / Space on the clickable rows; 320 px toolbar wrap and 24 px tab
  closes; 💸 the floating Brahma console over a modal; the `qt-checkbox`
  Automation row under Madman's Box); **the vendor set** (the Guide lists 137
  pages with `Diagrams` and `Storyteller Mode` in place; `help_docs` 135 →
  137 rows on first boot with 35 `updatedAt` moves — 💸 the re-embed only if
  a profile is configured; the six theme previews showing the pin's tokens).
- **§S.9 — The gated beats flip LIVE.** P4.D344 / P4.D346 / P4.D352 /
  P4.D353 / P4.D359 author their Playwright beats behind gate constants
  (`const P4D343_SERVER_LANDED = false` etc.), with throwaway stand-ins
  deleted at the flip. On the union the unifier flips each to `true`,
  deletes the stand-ins, runs each touched spec by file (alone; 4353–4359
  free), then the full suite. A red is root-caused against the server logs
  before it is accepted as the recorded timing class.

## Shared contracts (binding; name-level — the long forms are the surveys' §7 and, where an order is finished, that order's contract section)

  - **C-1 — the keystone reads (consumed by P4.D342):** `db::memories_read::

    `count_keystones(conn, character_id, about_character_id: Option<&str>,

- **C2 — the Storyteller wire W1 (P4.D343 builds, P4.D344 consumes;
  `crates/quilltap-core/src/api/types.rs` + `apps/web/src/app/core/core-
  contract.ts`):** `Request::ChatUpdate { …, turn_mode: Option<Option<Value>>
  }` (`double_option`, serde key `turnMode`, a sibling of `chat`; absent →
  untouched; `"rotation"` / `"storyteller"` → `set_turn_mode` (+ `abandon_
  storyteller_round(chat_id, "abandoned")` on leaving); `null` or anything
  else → `BadRequest "Validation error"`, nothing written) ↔ `ChatUpdate
  Request.turnMode?: 'rotation' | 'storyteller'`; the bag key `chat.
  storytellerInviteFilter: boolean` (non-boolean → the whole PUT refused).
  `Request::ChatStorytellerRound { chat_id }` → `Response::ChatStoryteller
  Round(Value)` = `{ "round": StorytellerRound | null }`. `Request::Chat
  StorytellerRun { chat_id, round_id: String, participant_ids: Vec<String>,
  order: Option<String>, keep_order: Option<bool> }` (keys `roundId`,
  `participantIds`, `order` default `"sequential"`, `keepOrder`; a non-enum
  `order` → `BadRequest "Invalid request"`) → an EMPTY reply (`Response::Ok`
  / `Null` — the web edge answers the SSE stream; `{ ended }` would be extra
  information v4 never answers); refusals `Conflict` 409 `That round is no
  longer open`, `NotFound` 404 `That seat is not on this round` / `Chat not
  found`, `BadRequest` 400 `Choose at least one bid, or let the round lie` /
  `Only a seat that bid to speak can be chosen` / `A Destroy bid must be
  chosen on its own`. `Request::ChatStorytellerAsk { chat_id, round_id,
  participant_id }` → `Response::ChatStorytellerAsk(Value)` = `{ "bid":
  StorytellerBid }` (the ANSWERED card). `Request::ChatStorytellerLie {
  chat_id, round_id }` → `{ "closed": true }`. Frames on `EventPayload::Chat
  (ChatEvent)`: `{"storytellerRound": StorytellerRound}`, `{"storytellerBid":
  {"roundId", "bid": StorytellerBid}}`, `{"roundOpen": true, "roundId"}`,
  `chainComplete` with `reason: "round_open"` (the literal; no enum change);
  a concurrent turn's frames carry `"participantId"` (when the frame lacked
  one) and `"concurrent": true` as the LAST two keys (`ChatEvent::Tagged`
  flattening inner then the two keys). The chat GET wire: `turnMode:
  'rotation' | 'storyteller'` (never null on the wire), `storytellerInvite
  Filter: boolean`, both after `isPaused`. Realtime topic `"storytellerRounds"`
  scoped by chat id → `chatKeys.storytellerRound(chatId) = ['chat', chatId,
  'storytellerRound']`. `LlmLogDto.storytellerRoundId?: string | null`; log
  type `"STORYTELLER_BID"`. `CustomToolLibraryEntry.effect: 'read' | 'write'`
  after `intervention` (T2). TS types `StorytellerBid` (key order
  `participantId, characterId, characterName, status, statedTier?, tier?,
  tierRaised, tools, plan?, reason?, talkativeness, silent, askedAt?,
  answeredAt?`), `StorytellerRound` (`id, chatId, triggerMessageId, status,
  bids, selection, closedReason, createdAt, updatedAt, closedAt`),
  `StorytellerSelection { participantIds, order: Order | null, chosenAt,
  names }`, `StorytellerBidRecord { roundId, tier, plan, toolsNamed }`,
  `StorytellerTier` (`simple | lookup | act | destroy`), `StorytellerOrder`
  (`sequential | simultaneous`), `TIER_LABELS` — field for field, optional /
  nullable preserved. P4.D344 codes to this with `it.skipIf(SERVER_LANDED)`
  gates.
- **C3 — the intervention wire `InterventionWire` (P4.D345 builds, P4.D346
  consumes; the BINDING copy is P4.D345's order):** dispatch verbs
  `{"type":"chatCustomToolInterventionsList","chatId"}` → `Response::
  CustomToolInterventions(Value)` = `{"holds": InterventionHoldView[]}`;
  `{"type":"chatCustomToolInterventionPreview","chatId","id","overrides"}` →
  `Response::CustomToolInterventionPreview(Value)` = `{"preview":
  InterventionPreview}`; `{"type":"chatCustomToolInterventionReask","chatId",
  "id","overrides"}` → `Response::CustomToolInterventionReask(Value)` =
  `{"preview", "consults": [{ok, output, prompt}]}`; `{"type":"chatCustom
  ToolInterventionResolve","chatId","id","decision"}` → `Response::CustomTool
  InterventionResolve(Value)` = `{"resolved": "deal" | "as-rolled" | "veto"}`.
  Errors: 404 `Held run not found`; 409 `That run has already been settled`;
  400 — `This tool consults no oracle`, every `checkOverrides` sentence
  (`parameter "${name}" is not open to intervention`; `the roll is not open
  to intervention`; `a dice roll is set by its faces, not a number`; `this
  roll is not dice`; `this roll takes ${diceCount} dice, not ${n}`; `the
  ${field} is not open to intervention`; `there is no outcome row ${i}`;
  `effect ${i} is not open to intervention`), `CustomToolRunError`'s message,
  the Zod issue sentences joined `; `, and at the HTTP edge `The request body
  is not JSON`. HTTP: `GET /api/v1/chats/{id}/custom-tools?action=
  interventions`; `POST …?action=intervention-preview | intervention-reask |
  intervention-resolve` with bodies `{id, overrides}` / `{id, overrides}` /
  `{id, decision}`; an unknown action → the existing `Unknown action: <x>`
  envelope. Shapes (key order as v4's `view.ts` / `interventions.ts`; absent
  optionals OMITTED): `InterventionHoldView {id, tool, title, characterName:
  string|null, entrance: "llm"|"trigger", triggerPhrase?, createdAt: ms,
  eligible: {fields: string[], params: string[], effects: number[]},
  hasCharacter, definition: {parameters, outcomes: [{when, state, message,
  brief?}], effects, revealOdds, hasLlm, diceSides?}, baseline: Intervention
  Preview, consults: [{ok, output, prompt}]}`; `InterventionPreview {run:
  InterventionRunView, staleAnswer, promptNow?, clampedParams: string[],
  warnings: string[]}`; `InterventionRunView {params, rollForm, notation?,
  raw, diceRolls?, diceBreakdown, rollSpan: RunSpan, value, valueSpan: RunSpan,
  outcomeIndex, tableOutcomeIndex?, state, message, chipLabel?, title,
  visibility, announcer: "pascal"|"character", llm?: {ok, output, prompt},
  effects: InterventionEffectView[]}`; `InterventionEffectView {index, kind:
  "write"|"wardrobe"|"inventory"|"log", label, fired, skipped?, value?,
  items?, log?}`; `RunSpan {min, max, minExclusive?: true, maxExclusive?:
  true}`; `InterventionOverrides {params?, roll?, dice?: number[], value?,
  llmPrompt?, llmAnswer?: {ok, output}, outcomeIndex?, message?, state?,
  chipLabel?, title?, visibility?, announcer?, effects?: Record<"<index>",
  EffectPin>}`; `EffectPin {mode: "auto"|"on"|"off", value?, items?:
  string[], log?}`; the decision `{kind: "deal", overrides} | {kind:
  "as-rolled"} | {kind: "veto"}`. Event: `RealtimeTopic::CustomToolInterventions`
  = `"customToolInterventions"`, scoped by chat id, on hold add and on settle;
  SPA key `customToolsKeys.interventions(chatId) = ['customTools', chatId,
  'interventions']` (v5's prefix) + `customToolsKeys.all` in `ALL_REALTIME_
  PREFIXES`. `pascalMeta.intervention` (the LAST key of `pascalMeta`):
  `{resolvedAt: ISO, decision: "deal", changed: string[], dealt: {params, raw,
  diceRolls?, value, outcomeIndex, state, message, chipLabel?, toolTitle,
  visibility, announcer, llm?, effects?: [{index, fired, target, next?}]},
  reasks?, staleAnswer?: true}` — absent when never held or let stand as
  rolled; `PascalMetaIn.intervention: Option<serde_json::Value>` (opaque).
  The run result on every wire that spreads it (the chat POST `result`, the
  Workbench preview / `BenchRoll`): `…, llm?, chipLabel?, effects?, draw:
  {form:"range", unit} | {form:"dice", faces}, rollSpan, valueSpan,
  llmPromptRendered?, tableOutcomeIndex?`. The Workbench library entry
  `intervention: boolean` after `triggerCount` (before `effect`). The
  chat-settings key `customToolIntervention?: boolean` (GET / PUT) — P4.D341

- **C9 — the WCAG rules R1–R12 (P4.D358 writes; EVERY SPA lane applies to
  the files it owns and records a HANDOFF for the census):**
  - **R1 Modal dialog surface** (`dff76ebef`): the dialog CARD (never the
    overlay) carries `role="dialog"` — `role="alertdialog"` where the
    dialog-roles table says so — `aria-modal="true"`, `aria-labelledby=
    "<titleId>"` with `id="<titleId>"` on the visible title element (a
    per-instance id: `inject(UniqueId)` if the SPA has one, else a module
    counter `qt-dialog-<n>`); `tabindex="-1"`; the shared focus directive
    (`qtDialogFocus`, P4.D358's `ui/dialog-focus.ts`) for focus-in / Tab-trap
    / restore; Escape closes unless the host's busy gate says otherwise (keep
    v4's gate expression). For a `qt-modal` host: nothing — the primitive
    does it.
  - **R2 Non-modal floating surface** (`FloatingDialog`): `role="dialog"
    aria-modal="false" aria-labelledby`, `tabindex="-1"`, focus in on open
    and back to the opener on close, NO Tab trap, close button `aria-label=
    "Close"`, Escape inside closes it (topmost: `stopPropagation`), Escape
    elsewhere closes it only while `!document.querySelector('[aria-modal=
    "true"]')`, both skipping `defaultPrevented`.
  - **R3 Icon-only buttons** carry v4's `aria-label` byte: `Close`, `Cancel`,
    `Dismiss message`, `Close gallery`, `` Remove alias ${alias} ``, `Up one
    folder`, `Toggle model list` (+ `aria-expanded`).
  - **R4 Visible label ↔ control:** `<label for="<id>">` + `id="<id>"` on the
    control; never a bare `<span>` caption beside a control.
  - **R5 Heading-captioned control:** `aria-labelledby="<headingId>"` with
    `id` on the heading (ProfilesTab / ImageGenerationCard / ModelBehaviorCard
    / FieldSelectionStep / SuggestionCard / ContextCompression switch).
  - **R6 Placeholder-only / caption-less control:** `aria-label` with v4's
    string (never invent a string; where v4 has none, do not add one).
  - **R7 Switch:** `role="switch"` + `aria-checked` + a name (`aria-label` or
    `aria-labelledby`); the Prospero one is `type="button"` too.
  - **R8 Clickable non-button:** `role="button" tabindex="0" (keydown)=
    "activateOnKey($event)"` (P4.D358's `ui/activate-on-key.ts`) with
    `aria-expanded` (FileTable) / `aria-pressed` (gallery tile, quick-hide
    tags) where v4 sets them. Prefer a real `<button>` where the layout
    allows (v4 kept spans only inside `<tr>` / heading contexts and under
    the uppercase-button themes).
  - **R9 Live region for notices:** the toast container `role="status"
    aria-live="polite" data-toast-container`; an error toast `role="alert"`
    (P4.D358 only).
  - **R10 Colour-only state gets words:** `<span class="sr-only">Unmoderated
    chat.</span>`; ` (overheard)` appended to the whisper label.
  - **R11 Async result announced:** `<span class="sr-only" role="status"
    aria-live="polite">…</span>` (DocumentPane: `editingIndicator ??
    (saveLabel === 'Saved' ? 'Saved' : '')`).
  - **R12 Pointer targets ≥ 24 px** — CSS, P4.D357 (`.qt-tab-close`);
    templates: `min-w-6 min-h-6` on the inline copy-id button (P4.D358).
  The `e2e/support/toasts.ts` helper: `export const toastStack = (page:

  std::io::Result<String>; }` on `SyncDeps` (default `ProcMountInfo` reading

  (`"TARGET_NOT_PERSISTENT"`, `is_conflict() == false` → 400 with the byte-

  not on a bind mount, so anything written there is lost when the container
  is recreated. Choose a path under the data directory (/app/quilltap) or
  under a store that is bind-mounted from the host (see 'quilltap docs
  docker-mounts').`); `is_docker` from `services/data_dir::is_docker_
  environment`; `browse_directory(path, default_dir)` with the ONE `engine.
  rs:4479-4481` line; `DocumentTextExtractor::is_available(&self) -> bool {

  is `defaultChatSettings` — the ONE default source (the keystone adds its

  field's WHOLE default from it (per top-level `path[0]`), `null` where the
  column's schema accepts null, else drops the key; `find_one_where` by user
  orders `updatedAt DESC` and scans for the first valid row; `update_for_
  user_returning_id` SETs the merged validated row.
